儒鴻 (1476 TT)  Trading Buy

營運穩定成長，長期展望正向。  
投資建議 ：預估儒鴻 2026 年營收為 408.71 億元， YoY+7.59% ，
稅後 EPS 24.79 元。 2027 年預期在產能稼動維持高檔及客戶訂
單穩定下 ， 營收成長至424.43 億元 ，YoY+3.84% ，稅 後EPS 25.52
元。考量： (1)3Q26 毛利率如預期的順利回升，預期未來幾個季
度的毛利率可維持現有水準。 (2) 持續投入自動化設備更新提高
生產效率，並擴建新產能因應客戶需求，印尼新廠預計 4Q27 投
產，推動長線的成長動能。綜上所述，對儒鴻的投資建議為
Trading Buy ，目標價 385 元 (2027 年 PER 15.5X) 。  
預估 4Q26 營收維持成長，稅後 EPS 6.09 元： 儒鴻 3Q26 營收
維持高檔為 105.72 億元， YoY+9.74% ， QoQ+4.82% ，毛利率亦
受惠客戶新產品出貨增加，產品組合優化及匯率相對友善等因
素，順利提升至 31.18% ，稅後 EPS 為 6.36 元，符合預期。預期
4Q26 由於成衣價格上漲且客戶新品訂單持續出貨，可推動儒鴻
營收維持成長，預估儒鴻 4Q26 營收為 105.93 億元，
YoY+14.34% ，QoQ+0.20% ，毛利率 31.23% ，稅後 EPS 6.09 元。 
美國期中選舉結束，市場不確定因素消除，儒鴻訂單能見度維
持六個月左右 ：美國期中選舉在民主黨獲勝的結果下，可預期
極端的關稅政策不會再發生，不確定性因素逐漸消除，不過目
前也沒看到品牌端有追加訂單的動作出現，預期未來幾個月的
訂單及終端銷售會處於正常的水準。目前儒鴻的訂單能見度維
持在六個月左右，市場庫存也在相對健康水準，整體市況穩定。  
儒鴻持續投入自動化設備更新提高生產效率，並擴建新產能因
應客戶需求，印尼新廠預計 4Q27 投產：儒鴻持續擴增新產能及
自動化設備更新以因應客戶需求，除了在台灣、越南二地的生
產設備持續更新外，印尼廠新產能也計劃在 4Q27 產能投產。目
前印尼廠的產能利用率維持滿載水準，印尼廠的生產需求未來
的成長空間仍大，預期等印尼廠三期投產後，可為儒鴻帶來下
一段成長的動能。

### Page 2

個股報告  
2026 年 11 月 11 日

2 群益投顧 
  儒鴻的 ESG ：  
儒鴻 2016 年即制定溫室氣體盤查管理程序，並依循「儒鴻企業環安衛及能
源政策」設置溫室氣體盤查推行委員會，由董事長室擔任主任委員，推動廠
區溫室氣體盤查，並以 2017 年作為公司溫室氣體盤查的基準年，推動溫室
氣體減量。此外，致力於用水回收，除了在製程蒸氣冷凝回收使用外，更引
進新技術改善廢水回收。  
公司簡介：  
儒鴻為台灣主要紡織業者，具染整、織布、成衣上下游一貫生產能力，主要
產品為各式成衣，並生產機能布料應用在各類運動休閒服飾上。國際各知名
服飾品牌及通路商如 NIKE 、 UA、 LULULEMON 、 TARGET 、 COSTCO 皆
為儒鴻的客戶。  
預估 4Q26 營收維持成長，稅後 EPS 6.09 元：  
儒鴻 3Q26 營收維持高檔為 105.72 億元， YoY+9.74% ，QoQ+4.82% ，毛利率
亦受惠客戶新產品出貨增加，產品組合優化及匯率相對友善等因素，順利提
升至 31.18% ，稅後 EPS 為 6.36 元，符合預期。  
預期 4Q26 由於成衣價格上漲且客戶新品訂單持續出貨，可推動儒鴻營收維
持成長，預估儒鴻 4Q26 營收為 105.93 億元， YoY+14.34% ， QoQ+0.20% ，
毛利率 31.23% ，稅後 EPS 6.09 元。  
美國期中選舉結束，市場不確定因素消除，儒鴻訂單能見度維持六個月左
右：  
美國期中選舉在民主黨獲勝的結果下，可預期極端的關稅政策不會再發生，
不確定性因素逐漸消除，不過目前也沒看到品牌端有追加訂單的動作出現，
預期未來幾個月的訂單及終端銷售會處於正常的水準。目前儒鴻的訂單能
見度維持在六個月左右，市場庫存也在相對健康水準，整體市況穩定。  
儒鴻持續投入自動化設備更新提高生產效率，並擴建新產能因應客戶需求，
印尼新廠預計 4Q27 投產：  
儒鴻持續擴增新產能及自動化設備更新以因應客戶需求，除了在台灣、越南
二地的生產設備持續更新外，印尼廠新產能也計劃在 4Q27 產能投產。目前
印尼廠的產能利用率維持滿載水準，印尼廠的生產需求未來的成長空間仍
大，預期等印尼廠三期投產後，可為儒鴻帶來下一段成長的動能。  
投資建議：  
預估儒鴻 2026 年營收為 408.71 億元， YoY+7.59% ，稅後 EPS 24.79 元。2027
年預期在產能稼動維持高檔及客戶訂單穩定下，營收成長至 424.43 億元，
YoY+3.84% ，稅後 EPS 25.52 元。考量： (1)3Q26 毛利率如預期的順利回升，
預期未來幾個季度的毛利率可維持現有水準。 (2) 持續投入自動化設備更新
提高生產效率，並擴建新產能因應客戶需求，印尼新廠預計 4Q27 投產，推
動長線的成長動能。綜上所述，對儒鴻的投資建議為 Trading Buy ，目標價
385 元 (2027 年 PER 15.5X) 。

個股報告

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

## Document 025: 158円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 ～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の“三つの分岐点”が方向性を決める～

Source PDF: all report pdf/158円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 ～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の“三つの分岐点”が方向性を決める～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Investment Navigator

2026 年 10 月 22 日

1/6 
158 円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 
～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の"三つの分岐点"が方向性を決める～ 
 経済調査部 シニア・フェロー  嶌峰 義清 
（℡：050-5474-7489）

（要旨） 
○ ドル円相場は、7 月末の日米協調介入、9 月上旬の急激な円高、9 月の日米同時利上げを経て、
10 月に入ってからは1 ドル＝158 円前後の狭いレンジでの推移が続いている。円先物市場に
おける非商業部門の建玉は、9 月前半に過去に例のない規模で円の売り越しから買い越しへ
と転じた。しかし、その後は買い越し幅が縮小し、売建玉が再び増え始めている。投機筋の
円買い意欲は弱まりつつあると言えよう。 
○ 期待インフレ率 （BEI） で実質化した日米の実質金利差は、政策金利・2 年債・10 年債のいず
れでも 2％ポイントを超える米国優位が続いている。名目金利差は縮小したものの、米国の
金利上昇は実質金利の上昇、日本のは期待インフレ率の上昇によるところが大きい。このた
め、実質ベースのドル優位は崩れていない。 
○ 当面の焦点は、①11 月 3 日の米中間選挙、②選挙後のイラン情勢、③日銀の 12 月利上げと
その後のペース、の三つである。民主党が上下両院を制し、イラン情勢が沈静化に向かい、
日銀が利上げ路線を明確にすれば、円高方向への転換が視野に入る。逆の組み合わせとなれ
ば、再び160 円台を試し、介入への警戒が強まる展開も否定できない。

1. 投機筋の円ポジションは"円売り一色"から変化し、158 円を挟んで膠着が続くドル円相場 
ドル円相場は、方向感を欠いた推移が続いている。10 月入り後の値動きは、高値が158.94 円
（10 月14 日） 、安値が156.99 円（10 月2 日）と、月間の値幅は2 円弱にとどまる。 
もっとも、ここに至るまでの道のりは平坦ではなかった。4 月末から5 月の大型連休中には、
160 円台後半まで進んだ円安に対して、政府・日銀が総額11.7 兆円の円買い介入を実施した。そ
れでも円安基調は止まらず、7 月 28 日には163.91 円（ECB参照値）と、約40 年ぶりの円安水準を
つけた。これに対し、7 月 31 日には日米当局が28 年ぶりとなる円買い協調介入に踏み切った。介
入額は 15 兆3993 億円に達し、年初からの累計は27 兆 1342 億円と暦年で過去最大を更新した。 
その後は円高へと自律的な反発も見られた。9 月2～3 日には、日銀の追加利上げ観測の強まり
や米雇用関連指標の下振れを受けてドル円は1 日で3 円以上下落、9 日には153 円台まで円高が進
んだ。この局面では介入実績は無く、ジャクソンホール会議でのウォーシュFRB議長の発言をきっ

### Page 2

2/6 
かけに積み上がったドル買い・円売りの持ち高が、損切りを迫られた結果と考えられる。 
ところが、その後は再び円安に押し戻された。FRBは 9 月 16 日に約3 年ぶりの利上げに踏み切
り、政策金利を3.75～4.00％とした。日銀も18 日に政策金利を1.25％へ引き上げたものの、日
米がそろって利上げしたことで金利差は大きく変わらず、ドル円は158 円台へと戻っている。 
為替市場における"投機的な動き"の指標とされるのが、円先物市場における非商業部門（主に
ヘッジファンドなどとされる）の建玉である。その推移を見ると、ここ数ヶ月で大きな変化が生
じていることがわかる（図表1）。 
6 月 2 日時点の円売建玉は24 万 4416 枚と、円キャリートレードがブームだった2007 年以来の
高水準に達していた。差引（ネット）でも12 万9567 枚の売り越しだった。その後も、9 月1 日時
点では 9 万2227 枚の売り越しと、円売り超の状態が続いていた。 
しかし、9 月前半の急激な円高を境に状況は一変し、9 月 15 日時点では差引が12 万 0359 枚の
買い越しへと転じ、わずか2 週間で円買い方向に21 万枚強も振れた。この振れ幅は、統計をさか
のぼれる1986 年以降で最大である。同時点の円買建玉（23 万 7951 枚）も過去最大を記録した。 
もっとも、その後は買い越し幅の縮小が続いている。10 月13 日時点では、買建玉19 万 1256 枚
に対し売建玉14 万 1049 枚で、差引の買い越しは5 万0207 枚とピークの半分以下に縮んだ。売建
玉は 9 月末の12 万枚台から14 万枚台へと再び増えており、円売りの持ち高が積み上がり始めて
いる兆しもうかがえる。投資主体別に見ると、レバレッジド・ファンドは10 月 13 日時点で3 万
3673 枚の円売り越しとなっており、ヘッジファンドの円買い意欲がすでに後退していることを示
している。 
足元の売建玉は、過去の介入局面のように当局が介入を急がなければならない水準にはない。
一方で、9 月に積み上がった円の買い持ちの解消そのものが、円安圧力として作用している面もあ
ると考えられる。

3/6
2.  実質金利差から見たドル円相場
為替相場の方向性を最終的に決めるのは、日米の金融政策と、それを反映した金利差である。
そこで、日米の実質金利差を、政策金利・2 年債・10 年債の3 つの年限で見てみる（図表2） 。こ
こでは、実質化に用いる物価として、市場が織り込む期待インフレ率であるBEI（ブレーク・イー
ブン・インフレ率、10 年債）を用いた。足元の消費者物価は、米国では原油高で押し上げられ、
日本では電気・ガス料金支援で押し下げられており、実勢を見誤りやすいためである。
名目で見ると、政策金利は米FF金利の誘導目標が3.75～4.00％、日本の無担保コール翌日物が
1.25％である。10 月 21 日時点の国債利回りは、米国が2 年債4.79％・10 年債 5.13％、日本が2
年債 1.886％・10 年債2.882％となっている。一方、10 年BEIは米国が2.35％（10 月20 日） 、日
本が 2.1～2.2％程度と、ほぼ同水準である。これを差し引いて実質化すると、筆者の試算ではお

おむね次のようになる。
✓ 政策金利：米国＋1.5～1.6％程度、日本▲0.9～1.0％程度（米国優位・差は約2.5％ポイント）
✓ 2 年債：米国＋2.4％程度、日本▲0.3％程度（米国優位・差は約2.7％ポイント）
✓ 10 年債：米国＋2.8％程度、日本＋0.7％程度（米国優位・差は約2.1％ポイント）
名目で見れば、日本の10 年債利回りが9 月下旬に3.115％と約 30 年ぶりの水準に達するなど、
日米金利差は縮小してきた。しかし、BEIで実質化すると景色は異なる。すべての年限で米国の実
質金利が2％ポイント以上も上回っており、ドル優位の構図はほとんど崩れていないのである。
その背景には、日米で金利上昇の"中身"が異なることがある。米国では、昨年末から長期金利
が約 1％ポイント上昇したが、BEIは 2.25％から2.35％へとほぼ横ばいにとどまった。名目金利の
注：BEIで実質化

### Page 4

4/6 
上昇は、ほぼそのまま実質金利の上昇となり、10 年物の実質利回りは9 月末に 2.93％と2008 年
以来の高水準に達した。これに対し日本では、BEIが過去最高圏の2％台まで上昇しており、名目
金利の上昇の相応の部分を期待インフレ率の上昇が占めている。実際、9 月の日銀の利上げ後も
BEIはむしろ上昇した。日本の金利上昇は"インフレ期待による金利上昇"の色彩が濃く、実質金利
の上昇は限られていると言えよう。 
日本の政策金利は、実質では依然として▲1％近いマイナス圏にあり、金融環境はなお緩和的
だ。日銀の利上げや介入にもかかわらず円安の流れが変わらなかったのは、こうした実質金利差
の構図で説明できる面が大きい。逆に言えば、日本の期待インフレ率が2％近辺で落ち着いたまま
利上げが進むか、米国の実質金利が低下に転じない限り、本格的な円高への転換は難しいと考え
られる。 
なお、日本の物価連動国債は発行残高が約10 兆円と小さく、BEIには流動性の影響も含まれ
る。政策金利や2 年債を10 年BEIで実質化するのも簡便的な手法である点には留意が必要だ。

3.  中間選挙の結果次第で変わるドルの行方 
11 月3 日には米国の中間選挙が行われる。1934 年以降の23 回の中間選挙のうち、現職大統領
の政党が上下両院とも議席を増やしたのはわずか2 回しかない。今回も、ロイター通信の世論調
査ではトランプ大統領の支持率が過去最低の31％まで落ち込み、投票先では民主党47％、共和党
35％と 12 ポイントの差がついている。選挙予測サイトのディシジョン・デスクHQは、民主党が下
院を制する確率を78％、上院を制する確率を56％と見ている。 
結果ごとに、ドル円相場への影響を整理すると次のようになる。 
① 民主党が上下両院を制する場合：予算や減税、関税政策に対する議会のチェックが強まり、
財政拡張への期待が後退することで、米長期金利の低下とドル安につながりやすい。FRBの人事に
対する議会の影響力が強まり、金融政策の独立性への懸念が和らぐことも、長期金利の押し下げ
要因となろう。一方で、予算案を巡る対立が先鋭化すれば、政府機関の閉鎖などを通じたリスク
回避の円買いが生じる可能性もある。いずれにしても、ドル円には下押し圧力がかかる公算が大
きい。 
② 下院は民主党、上院は共和党の"ねじれ"となる場合：現在の政策の大枠が維持される一方
で、新たな大型財政出動は難しくなる。相場の反応は限定的で、関心は再び日米の金融政策へと
移ることになろう。 
③ 共和党が両院の過半数を維持する場合：減税や規制緩和など拡張的な政策への期待から、米
長期金利の上昇とドル高が進みやすい。ドル円が再び160 円台を試し、介入への警戒が一気に高
まる展開も想定される。 
世論調査どおりであれば①ないし②の公算が大きく、ドル円にはどちらかと言えば円高寄りの
材料となろう。ただし、重要なのは選挙後の財政・通商政策や金利見通しの変化であり、選挙直
後の値動きだけで方向性を判断するのは早計だろう。

### Page 5

5/6 
4.  イラン情勢：選挙後の"重し"が外れる 
もう一つの大きな不透明要因がイラン情勢である。2 月 28 日に始まった米国・イスラエルとイ
ランの戦闘は、ホルムズ海峡の事実上の封鎖と、米国によるイラン港湾の海上封鎖を伴ったまま
長期化している。北海ブレント原油先物は20 日に一時1 バレル＝111.99 ドルと約5 ヶ月ぶりの高
値をつけた。 
原油高は、いくつかの経路でドル高円安に作用している。米国では、ガソリン価格の前年比
34％の上昇などを背景に、9 月の消費者物価が同＋3.7％へと加速し、FRBの追加利上げ観測を支え
ている。日本にとっては、エネルギー輸入額の増加を通じて貿易収支が悪化し、実需の円売りが
増える。加えて、地政学リスクの高まりは"有事のドル買い"も誘いやすい。 
注目されるのは、トランプ大統領が「中間選挙前にはイランを攻撃しない」と明言している点
だ。言い換えれば、選挙後はこの制約が外れることになる。選挙後に軍事行動が再開されれば、
原油価格の一段高と米利上げ観測の強まりを通じて、ドル高円安が進むリスクがある。逆に、仲
介国を通じた交渉がまとまり、海峡の再開に向かえば、原油価格の下落とともに米長期金利が低
下し、ドル安円高へと振れやすい。実際、20～21 日には原油安を受けて米長期金利が低下し、ド
ルが売られる場面が見られた。

5.  日米の金融政策：12 月の"同時利上げ"の先が焦点 
FRBは9 月に約3 年ぶりの利上げに踏み切り、年内にもう1 回の利上げを示唆した。10 月14 日
に9 月の消費者物価が前年比3.7％と予想を上回ると、27～28 日のFOMCでの利上げ確率は一時5
割前後まで上昇した。しかし、17 日にウォーシュ議長が10 月会合の判断を予断しない姿勢を示し
たことで、利上げ確率は1 割台半ばまで低下している。市場では「10 月は見送り、12 月に利上
げ」との見方が大勢となった。一部には、12 月が今回の利上げ局面で最後になるとの予想も出て
いる。 
一方、日銀の植田総裁は、9 月の利上げ後に「政策の局面は変化した」と述べ、その後も基調的
な物価上昇率が2％に「かなり近づいている」との認識を示した。そのうえで、 「これ以上上がる
ことは容認できない」とも踏み込んでいる。29～30 日の決定会合では政策金利が据え置かれる公
算が大きいが、基調物価が2％に到達したと判断されると報じられている。 
日米がともに12 月に利上げすれば、名目の政策金利差は変わらない。為替にとって重要なの
は、その先の方向性の違いである。FF金利が明確に中立水準を超えるFRBの利上げが最終局面に近
づく一方、日銀は中立金利を目指して利上げを続ける。この構図が鮮明になれば、日米の実質金
利差は徐々に縮小に向かおう。逆に、原油高を受けて米国の物価上昇が加速し、FRBが一段の利上
げを迫られる展開となれば、実質金利差の縮小は見込めず、ドル円相場の膠着状態を演出しよ
う。

6.  今後の見通し：年末に向けて緩やかな円高を想定、ただし分岐は大きい 
以上を踏まえると、当面のドル円相場は、日米の金融政策会合と中間選挙という大きなイベン

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
トを控え、当面は現状の狭いレンジでの推移が続くと考えられる。10 月27～28 日のFOMCは据え置
きが市場の大勢だ。FOMCで利上げが見送られ、その後の日銀会合で基調物価2％到達が確認されて
12 月利上げへの地ならしが進めば、円高方向に振れやすい組み合わせとなる。しかし、市場では
FRBも年内利上げを実施するとの見方が強く、ドル円相場を動かす材料とはなりにくい。逆に、
FRBが10 月のFOMCで利上げを決定した場合、ドル円相場は159 円を超える円安進展の可能性が高
まる。 
年末にかけては、①中間選挙で民主党が議会の主導権を握り、②イラン情勢が交渉による解決
に向かい原油価格が落ち着き、③日銀が12 月に利上げを実施して来年以降も利上げ継続の姿勢を
示す、という三つの条件がそろえば、日米の実質金利差は縮小に向かう。その場合は、150 円台半
ばから前半への円高が進む可能性がある。投機筋の売建玉が再び積み上がっている局面であれ
ば、その巻き戻しが円高の勢いを増幅することも考えられる。 
一方で、①共和党の善戦、②選挙後のイランへの軍事行動の再開、③FRBの利上げ継続＋日銀の
利上げ慎重姿勢打ち出し、といった組み合わせとなれば、ドル円は再び160 円台を試すことにな
ろう。その場合、政府・日銀は過去の介入水準である160 円台前半から164 円近辺を意識しつ
つ、円安のスピードを抑えるための介入に踏み切る公算が大きい。ただし、これまでの経験が示
すように、ファンダメンタルズが変わらない中での介入は"時間を買う"政策にすぎず、効果が一
時的にとどまる可能性には注意が必要だ。 
筆者は、①米中間選挙は最低でも上下院のねじれ、場合によっては上院においても民主党の過
半数獲得、②選挙後に米国がイランへの軍事行動を再開、同時に仲介国を介した交渉を再開する
ものの、膠着状態が続く、③日米とも利上げ継続、をメインシナリオとしている。 
さらにリスク要因として、①米中間選挙の結果がなかなか確定せず、議会勢力の確定に時間が
かかる、②イラン情勢はフーシ派から攻撃を受けているサウジアラビアや、総選挙を終えたイス
ラエルによる本格的な攻撃参戦、の可能性を挙げる。 
このように不透明要因が多いことも足元のドル円相場の膠着の要因になっていると考えられる
が、短期的な値動きに一喜一憂するよりも、実質金利差という基本的な決定要因がどちらに動い
ているかを冷静に見極めることが重要であり、日米中央銀行の金融政策姿勢が最も注目される。

以 上

みずほ経済 EXPRESS

みずほ GDP ナウ（2026 年 10 月中旬時点） 
～7～9 月期 GDP は前期比＋0.03％（年率＋0.10％）と推計～

調査部  シニア日本経済エコノミスト 越山祐資 
同             エコノミスト  阿部大樹 
080-1069-4835 yusuke.koshiyama@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 10 月中旬時点の「みずほ GDP ナウ」は 7～9 月期 GDP を前期比＋0.10％と予測 
みずほ総合研究所では景気動向をタイムリーに把握するべく、浦沢（2023）等を参考にGDPナウキ
ャスティングに取り組んできた。これを「みずほGDPナウ」と呼称することとし、太田他（2024）で
はデータドリブン予測である「みずほGDPナウ」が民間予測平均並みの予測精度を確保できることを
示した。その上で、酒井他（2024）をはじめとして、月次経済指標を用いたGDPナウキャスティング
の結果を紹介してきたところである。 
本稿では10月中旬時点までに得られる月次経済指標を用い、 「みずほGDPナウ」 による7～9月期GDP
の予測結果を紹介する。予測に用いたデータは、8月分の鉱工業生産、 消費活動指数、 所定外労働時間、
消費財出荷指数、第3次産業活動指数、9月分の中小企業景況調査（売上げ見通しDI）である1。図表1
には、予測に使用した各月次経済指標の推移を示している。

2

モデルによる10月中旬時点における7 ～9月期実質GDP成長率の予測値は、図表2のとおり前期比＋
0.03％ （年率＋0.10％）となった。 他機関による予測では、東京財団政策研究所による10月15日時点の
ナウキャスティングが同＋0.29％（年率＋1.18％）、日本経済研究センターが公表した10月のESPフォ
ーキャスト調査（回答期間：2026年9月30日～10月7日、回答者37名）における民間予測値平均が前期
比年率＋0.08％となっている。「みずほGDPナウ」の予測値は、民間コンセンサスよりやや高いもの
の、概ね近い伸びを予測していると言える。 
なお、多くの民間エコノミストが保守的な見方をしている理由の一つには、GDP統計作成上のテク
ニカルな要因がある。4～6月期には、 中東情勢悪化を背景に原油 ・ ナフサ輸入が大幅に減少したほか、
石油化学製品の供給途絶懸念から幅広い企業で在庫積み増しの動きが生じた。やや直感に反するが、
いずれもGDPに対しては押し上げ要因となる。 そのため、4～6月期GDPは前期比＋0.36％ （年率＋1.45％）
とプラス成長になったものの、 成長率が示すほど景気の実態は強くなかったとみるのが自然であろう。
反対に、7～9月期については、4～6月期の反動で輸入増・在庫減の動きが顕在化し、GDPには一定の
下方圧力がかかる可能性がある。みずほ総合研究所が9月に公表したレポート2でも、こうしたテクニ
カル要因を考慮の上、7～9月期GDPは横ばい圏の伸びにとどまると予想している。 
■ 「みずほ GDP ナウ」による予測値下方修正の主因は生産・消費関連指標の悪化 
「みずほGDPナウ」 による7～9月期のGDP（1次速報値） の予測にあたっては10月中旬までに公表さ
れた月次経済指標を使って予測値を4回更新した。図表3では、予測値の更新過程と各月次経済指標の
寄与度を示している3。予測値が下方修正された主因は、8月の鉱工業生産、 消費財出荷指数の低下だ。
特に鉱工業生産指数については、7月末に発生した熊本地震による影響が下押し要因になったとみら
れる。 大手自動車メーカーで工場の操業が停止した影響で自動車が前月比▲6.8％と大幅に減産したほ
か、 電子部品 ・ デバイスも同▲1.9％の減産となった。7月調査時点の生産予測指数は前月比＋3.1％（ 補
図表 2 各機関の実質GDP成長率予測値 図表 3 予測値の改定過程と各経済指標の寄与度

### Page 3

3

正値ベース）と増産が見込まれていたが、大幅に下振れた格好だ。一方、世界的に旺盛なAI・データ
センター関連需要を背景に、半導体製造装置を含む生産用機械などが増産したものの、熊本地震の影
響を打ち返せなかった。消費財出荷指数の低下については、4～5月の耐久財特需が一巡したことで反
動減の動きが生じた。非耐久財の出荷は前月から増加したものの、7月の落ち込みも踏まえると、 基調
としては力強さを欠いていると言えよう。長雨や低温、といった天候不順により生鮮野菜価格が上昇
しているほか、食肉も値上がりが大きく、こうした手に取る機会が多い品目の価格上昇が家計の節約
志向を強め、個人消費の下押し圧力となっているとみられる。9月についても、食品のほか、原油・石
油化学製品等のコスト高の影響で幅広い品目の価格上昇が加速する見込みであり、個人消費は冴えな
い動きが続く可能性がある。 
以上のように、7～9月期成長率は低い伸びに抑えられる公算が大きい。前述のとおり原油・ナフサ
の輸入増、 企業の在庫調整といった動きはGDP統計上、成長率に対してマイナスに寄与する。加えて、
熊本地震による生産減や天候要因による消費支出抑制の動きが下押し要因になる見込みだ。 
次回の 「みずほGDPナウ」については、11月16日に公表される7～9月期GDP1次速報を挟んで、10～
12月期GDPのリアルタイム予測に取り組む。10月分の鉱工業生産や消費活動指数の結果等を踏まえて
推計し、12月中旬頃のレポート発刊を予定している。原油高等を受けた物価上昇圧力の強まりや堅調
なAI・データセンター関連需要など、プラス・マイナス双方の影響が交錯する中、ナウキャストによ
る景気動向の把握はより重要なものとなるだろう。

1 ステップワイズ法により使用データを採択した。詳細は太田ほか（2024）を参照されたい。今後のモデルの予測精度のパフォ
ーマンス評価等を踏まえ、採択するデータについては見直しを行う可能性がある。 
2 詳細については、服部直樹「年率＋1.4％と 1 次速報から小幅に上方修正（4～6 月期2 次QE）」（2026 年9 月8 日）を参照され
たい。 
3 太田他（2024）で示した枠組みと同様、図表 2 の折れ線が各時点における実質GDP 成長率の予測値であり、月次の経済指標が
新たに公表されたり改定されたりすることで予測値が更新される。棒グラフは、予測値の改定幅、すなわち前回予測との差を各
月次経済指標で寄与度分解したもので、寄与度を合計するとモデル予測値の改定幅と一致する。なお、 「みずほGDP ナウ」は各
月次経済指標についても公表前に予測値を計算しており、各指標の寄与度はモデル予測値と実績値の乖離から計算されることか
ら、寄与度の符号は経済指標の変化の方向とは必ずしも一致しない。

華邦電子是利基型記憶體與積體電路解決方案供應商，核心產品包括 DRAM、NOR 
Flash、NAND Flash 以及邏輯 IC 產品，廣泛應用於手持裝置、消費性電子、電腦周
邊 、 車用與工業電子等市場 。2026 年產品組合為 CMS 59%、Flash31%、Logic 10%。

➢ 3Q26 報價漲幅強勁，惟位元出貨量下滑 
3Q26 營收 823.3 億元，QoQ+37.58%，YoY+278.18%，成長動能主要
仍來自於記憶體短缺所帶來的報價強勁漲幅，惟公司為因應 LTA 需求而
實行嚴格控貨策略，導致出貨量呈現下滑。獲利表現方面，3Q26 毛利率
約 70.57%，QoQ+4.32ppts，YoY+23.88ppts， 稅後淨利354.22 億元 ，
QoQ+45.67%，YoY+1,103.54%，EPS 為 7.58 元。  
➢ 4Q26 Flash 產品仍具強勁上漲動能，獲利表現可望持續提升 
展望 4Q26，預估 DRAM 產品 ASP 漲幅約逾 10%，NOR 產品漲幅可望
逾 20%。 預估4Q26 營收約 994.8 億元 ，QoQ+20.83%，YoY+273.64%，
毛利率 72.25%， 稅後淨利416 億元 ，QoQ+17.44%，YoY+1,115.55%，
EPS 為 8.81 元。  
➢ 2027 年 DRAM 及 Flash 新產能開出，挹注營收成長動能 
公司目標將高雄廠產能擴增至 24K，隨新產能於 2H26 投片，且新增產
能皆將採用 16nm 製程，逐步取代舊有的 25Snm 與部分 20nm 製程，
位元出貨量預估將會大幅增加。考量記憶體供需吃緊帶動報價漲幅優於
預期 ， 且隨著位元出貨量逐季成長 ， 投研部預估2026 年全年營收約 2799
億元，毛利率 67.89%，稅後淨利 1114.5 億元，EPS 為 24.04 元。展望
2027 年，預期全球市場將面臨更為緊俏且吃緊的供需格局，而受惠於約
5K 的產能擴充，Flash 2027 年的位元出貨量成長率可望突破 40%。
DRAM 方面 ， 高雄廠則預計滿載月產能將達24~25K， 並透過製程轉換 ，
提高 16nm 製程比重，進一步推成位元出貨量。投研部預估 2027 年營
收約 6,008 億 元 ， 毛 利 率71.21% ， 稅 後 淨 利 為2,550 億元，
YoY+128.84%，EPS 為 54.03 元。  
➢ CUBE 及矽電容陸續貢獻營收，中長期營運動能正向 
評價方面，考量公司 3Q26 ASP 漲勢優於先前預期，且 4Q26 Flash 產
品仍具上漲空間，再加上 2027 年位元出貨量可望迎來顯著成長，營收
成長動能強勁之餘，中長期亦有 CUBE 以及矽電容營收貢獻，預期營運
至 2028 年可望維持高成長態勢，故投研部維持強力買進建議，目標價
205 元(2027F PE 4X)。

個股報告

公司簡介 
華邦電子股份有限公司成立於 1987 年 9 月，公司在中科與南科高雄園區各設有一座
12 吋晶圓廠 。華邦電子是利基型記憶體與積體電路解決方案供應商 ， 同時擁有自有產
品及技術，從產品設計、研發、晶圓製造到品牌行銷完整垂直整合。核心產品包括
DRAM、NOR Flash、NAND Flash 以及邏輯 IC 產品，廣泛應用於手持裝置、消費性
電子 、 電腦周邊 、 車用與工業電子等市場 。2026 年產品組合為 CMS53%、Flash 35%、
Logic 11%。 
圖 1：華邦電產品組合

資料來源：公司、國票投顧預估 
3Q26 報價漲幅強勁，惟位元出貨量下滑 
3Q26 營收 823.3 億元，QoQ+37.58%，YoY+278.18%，營收成長動能主要仍來自
於記憶體短缺所帶來的報價強勁漲幅 ，惟營收仍低於先前預期 ， 主要因公司為因應 LTA
需求而策略性保留部分產能，實行嚴格控貨策略，導致產能利用率雖然滿載，出貨量
呈現下滑。產品報價漲幅方面皆優於預期，DRAM 產品 ASP 約季增 60%，Flash 產
品 ASP 則季增逾 30%；然而在位元出貨量方面，DRAM 出貨量約季減 8%，Flash 產
品則受惠於高容量 SLC/NOR 需求維持強勁，產能利用率滿載，出貨量與 2Q26 大致
持平。獲利表現方面，3Q26 毛利率約 70.57%，QoQ+4.32ppts，YoY+23.88ppts，
稅後淨利 354.22 億元，QoQ+45.67%，YoY+1,103.54%，EPS 為 7.58 元。 
4Q26 Flash 產品仍具強勁上漲動能，獲利表現可望持續提升 
展望 4Q26，雖然面臨消費性電子產品淡季修正，部分客戶在 4Q26 進入庫存調節，
然而 AI 伺服器、車用與 Edge AI 等領域的拉貨動能依然保持強勁。預估 DRAM 產品
ASP 漲幅約逾 10%，NOR 產品則受惠於 AI 伺服器對 512Mb 以上高容量 NOR Flash
的大量需求，短期缺口難以填補，市場報價仍具備繼續上行的空間，預估 ASP 漲幅仍
可望逾 20%。位元出貨量方面，預期 DRAM 可望受惠製程升級以及良率提升，出貨
量下滑幅度可望收斂，Flash 產品則可望在台中廠產能將針對 24 奈米進行小量擴產，
帶動位元出 貨量逐季 提升。預估 4Q26 營收約 994.8 億 元，QoQ+20.83% ，
YoY+273.64%，毛利率 72.25%，QoQ+1.68ppts，YoY+30.38ppts，稅後淨利 416
億元，QoQ+17.44%，YoY+1,115.55%，EPS 為 8.81 元。

2027 年 DRAM 及 Flash 新產能開出，挹注營收成長動能 
目前高雄廠月產能約為 15K，公司目標在年底前將 16nm 機台買足至 16K 的投片規
模，並計畫在年底前將高雄廠產能擴增至 24K，隨著新產能於 2H26 開始投片，且新
增產能皆將採用 16nm 製程，逐步取代舊有的 25Snm 與部分 20nm 製程，位元出貨
量預估將會大幅增加，預估 2H26 僅少量 16nm 產品將貢獻營收，預計 2027 年營收
貢獻將大幅提升。考量記憶體供需吃緊帶動報價漲幅優於預期，且隨著位元出貨量逐
季成長，投研部預估 2026 年全年營收約 2,799 億元，YoY+213%，毛利率 67.89%，
稅後淨利 1,114.5 億元，YoY+2713.11%，EPS 為 24.04 元。 
展望 2027 年，預期全球市場將面臨更為緊俏且吃緊的供需格局，而受惠於約 5K 的
產能擴充，Flash 2027 年的位元出貨量成長率可望突破 40%。此外，由於原廠陸續將
低容量 2D NAND EOL，促使低容量 eMMC 需求轉向 SLC NAND，使 SLC NAND 的
供給在 2027 年維持緊縮狀態。此外，在伺服器端，預估全新的AI 伺服器機櫃，單一
機櫃將採用達 500 顆至 600 顆的 NOR 與 NAND Flash 晶片，尤其拉動 512Mb、
1Gb 及 2Gb 等高容量快閃記憶體的出貨。DRAM 方面，預計 2027 年將增加來自於
Enterprise SSD 的營收貢獻，目前華邦電已經在進行送樣，看好 Enterprise SSD 需
求持續提升之下，可望為華邦電帶來額外 10%的 DRAM 營收貢獻。產能方面，如前
段所述，預計台中廠將額外擴增 5K Flash 產能，並且陸續降低 32nm 等舊製程投片
量，將產能轉移至較為先進的 Flash 製程；高雄廠則預計滿載月產能將達 24~25K，
並透過製程轉換，提高 16nm 製程比重，進一步推成位元出貨量。投研部預估 2027
年營收約 6,008 億元，YoY+114.64%，毛利率 71.21%，YoY+3.3ppts，稅後淨利為
2,550 億元，YoY+128.84%，EPS 為 54.03 元。 
展望後市，產品應用的層面將隨著 Edge AI、On-device AI 以及 Physical AI 的興起
而顯著擴張，尤其在邊緣端設備上，NOR Flash 及 NAND Flash 的容量與數量需求都
將出現爆發性成長；DRAM 則將隨著前述 eSSD 的需求增加而帶動出貨成長。為了因
應 2028 年後更為龐大的市場需求，華邦已經通過 731 億元的資本支出案，正式啟動
下一階段高雄廠 Module B 的擴產計畫。該擴建工程預計 2029 年 1 月開始裝機，預
計於 2H29 小量出貨。Module B 的無塵室面積約為目前高雄廠的兩倍，滿載產能可
望達 50K 至 60K 的月產能，預計將採分階段裝機投產。製程方面，Module B 將採用
16 奈米、14 奈米及 12 奈米製程，並規劃在第二階段引進 EUV。 
此外，針對矽電容佈局方面，公司目前具備 5,400 nF/mm²超高容值規格的技術，具
備極薄化、耐高溫與優異的高頻反應特性。面對 AI 晶片先進封裝空間緊湊及傳統
MLCC 缺貨之痛點，華邦電矽電容可直接嵌入PCB 或 CoWoS 中介層，滿足光模組與
AI 加速晶片對高頻穩定供電的需求。公司採 Foundry 定位，目前產能已被 MLCC 及
AI 供應商包光，並已於高雄廠 Module B 規劃專屬線擴產，預估 2027 年後將成為
CUBE 之外的重要動能。 
CUBE 及矽電容陸續貢獻營收，中長期營運動能正向 
整體而言 ， 隨著華邦電4Gb DDR4/DDR3 出貨占比提升 ， 以及3Q26 陸續出貨的 8Gb 
DDR4 加入，可望改善產品組合，配合報價漲勢提升獲利表現；Flash 方面除了前述
受供給面影響而使報價呈上行趨勢外，預期華邦電將持續維持全球第一大 NOR Flash
供應商的領導地位，並受惠於長期伺服器與 AI 高容量應用的滲透率提升，同時隨車
用 、 消費性電子產品規格升級 ， 對於NOR Flash 或 SLC NAND 的需求亦見同步增長 ，
故公司於 2026 年再度追加 NOR 及 NAND 的產能擴充資本支出，顯見其對於後市正

### Page 4

- 4 -

個股報告 
向看待；最後，在中長期方面，CUBE 目前已進入與客戶合作開發階段，主要針對 AI 
ASIC 及邊緣運算需求，其優勢在於採用3D 堆疊技術，4 層堆疊的頻寬可對標 HBM，
但功耗僅為其 20%，極具競爭力，預計將於 2027 年開始顯著貢獻營收，同時公司新
增矽電容業務，預計將成為公司長期成長的關鍵動能。 
評價方面，考量公司 3Q26 ASP 漲勢優於先前預期，且 4Q26 Flash 產品仍具上漲空
間，再加上 2027 年位元出貨量可望迎來顯著成長，營收成長動能強勁之餘，中長期
亦有 CUBE 以及矽電容營收貢獻，預期營運至 2028 年可望維持高成長態勢，故投研
部維持強力買進建議，目標價 205 元(2027F PE 4X)。

### Page 1

ab 5 November 2026
Global Research
First Read
Elan Microelectronics
Q426E likely to be the near-term trough
Q326 earnings below on softer NB demand environment
Elan's Q326 EPS of NT$0.58 (net profit NT$168mn) was below UBSe/street of NT
$1.29/1.71, as a result of lower revenue amid weaker NB demand environment. Q326
revenue was already reported at NT$2.2bn (-31% QoQ), below UBSe/street of NT
$2.4/2.8 bn as NB brand customers further reduced their orders from late-Aug on softer

end-demand after retail price hike and higher component inventories. GM was down
2.7ppts QoQ to 44.7% on less favourable mix, directionally in-line with UBSe of ~2ppt
QoQ decline. It booked non-op loss of NT$100mn, vs NT$294mn gain in Q226, mainly
due to unrealized loss on financial assets. Despite softer earnings, Elan remains
committed on its high dividend yield policy and is considering to raise its payout.
Near-term demand remains muted but Q426E likely to be the trough

Elan is restricted from providing Q4 financial guidance given the accumulated non-op
gain in past four quarters exceeded 10% of the net profit. With October sales reported
at NT$461 mn (-23% MoM), Elan expects Q426 remains a challenging quarter amid
softer demand environment and ongoing inventory adjustment. Nevertheless, we think
channel inventory is on track to be depleted in the coming months and Q426 is likely to
mark the trough for near-term revenue. We forecast Q426E sales to decline 38% QoQ in

Q426E but will rebound by 27% QoQ in Q127E on restocking. Into 2027, Elan noted it
has won some new projects at top NB brands, while also sees share gain opportunities
following its peer's business restructure, as well as ramping low-cost solution to capture
market trend. It also see good progress of haptics (2026 sales 5-6x vs 2025) and single
layer on-cell (3-4x), and we believe the momentum should continue into 2027.
ADAS and Drone remain key long-term growth drivers

Elan's AI-related revenue reached 14% of sales in Q326 and is expected to further
increase in Q426F as AI revenue remains more resilient vs NB-related products. Its ADAS
revenue is primarily driven by EV bus now and it thinks the proliferation into logistic
vehicles should be the next driver. Its drone-related business is on track to see higher
revenue contribution from 2027F as it has penetrated into Thunder Tiger's supply chain,
as well as two other customers, supplying both domestic and overseas demand. Elan

noted on top of the camera modules, its obstacle avoidance system, which carries much
higher ASP of US$800, could start to ramp from Q127F. We forecast its AI-business to
see a 45% sales CAGR in 2025-30E, outpacing traditional HMI.
Valuation: Maintain buy with a NT$150 price target
We cut 2026/27/28E EPS to NT$6.35/6.94/11.55 from NT$8.19/10.82/13.28 on softer
NB demand and prolonged inventory adjustment. We lower our PT to NT$150 (from NT
$170) using a lower multiple of 15x H227-H128E PE (from 16x 2027E P/E) to factor in

overall market valuation contraction. We view current headwinds as cyclical and believe
the market underappreciates Elan's AI opportunities and   upside from share gain.

Company Description
Elan Microelectronics is a Taiwan-based fabless IC design house specialising in human-
machine interface including touchscreen controllers, touchpad modules, pointing stick
modules and fingerprint sensors, specifically for notebook PCs. It started with
microcontrollers (MCUs) for PC peripherals and consumer electronics upon establishment in

1994, and further expanded the reach into notebook PC input devices through the
acquisition of a related subsidiary under KOA Corporation in 2003.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on a target PE multiple.
Fabless integrated circuit (IC) design houses face a variety of uncertainties, including rapidly
changing technology, end-market demand, competition, employee turnover and foundry
wafer costs. They are also vulnerable to legal disputes over intellectual property rights.

For Elan, we believe key risks include: 1) PC and NB demand dynamics, with any slowdown in
these end-markets directly impacting Elan's growth and also its new product roll-outs; 2) a
longer-than-expected product qualification cycle or slower-than-expected product adoption
among clients; 3) fiercer-than-expected price competition from global and Asian peers
negatively impacting growth and gross margin; and 5) faster-than-expected foundry and
assembly cost increases negatively impacting gross margin.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

## Document 195: 261110_ml_gce

Source PDF: all report pdf/261110_ml_gce.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Gold Circuit

Reiterate Buy: 3Q26 margin beat; ongoing 
sales/margin growth; trim PO to NT$1,250

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 1,250 TWD | Price: 983.00 TWD

3Q26 beat: solid margins despite lighter sales 
Gold Circuit (GCE)’s 3Q26 GM/OPM of 38.7%/30.6% exceeded BofAe of 35.9%/27.8% 
and consensus of 36.9%/28.9%, despite lighter sales (+22% QoQ, +67% YoY; 5%/3% 
below BofAe/consensus). OP (+48% QoQ, +95% YoY) was 4%/3% above 
BofAe/consensus. We attribute the strong core results to a still favorable mix and lower 
material cost burden. In 3Q26, AI made up 50%+ of sales, vs. 30-40%/40-50% in 
1Q/2Q26. Server/networking comprised 77%/17% of 3Q26 sales, vs. 72-78%/15-20% in 
1Q-2Q26. With a non-operating loss (details to be disclosed; likely FX loss in our view) 
amid a lighter tax rate, NP was 9%/7% above BofAe/consensus (Exhibit 3).  
Sales momentum intact; margin expansion continues 
We now expect 4Q26/1Q27 sales to rise 2%/3% QoQ & 2027/28 sales to rise 41%/31% 
YoY, driven by ASP growth (for spec upgrade and higher material cost) and ongoing 
capacity expansion in Taiwan/Thailand/China. GCE’s Oct sales (+6% MoM, +76% YoY) 
reinforce our confidence in its order and project pipeline. We also note that the impacts 
from AWS’ project pipeline change and shipping disruptions due to the flood in Thailand 
are fading. We now assume GM to expand from 36.8% in 2026E to 38.0%/38.3% in 
2027/28E, after considering higher material cost pass-through, higher depreciation costs 
from new facilities, and higher upfront costs for making next-generation HDI products.  
Cut PO to NT$1,250 with a lower P/E set-up; reiterate Buy 
We trim 2026-27E EPS by 1-4% but lift 2028E EPS by 4%, mainly to reflect the 3Q26 
results beat, stronger margin profile, but higher share count. We lower our PO to 
NT$1,250 (21x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,500 (25x 2027E EPS), with the valuation base 
unchanged and a lower P/E multiple. We apply 21x (c.1.5SD above P/E average since 
2020), vs. prior 25x (2.5SD above) on valuation de-rating across AI supply chain amid 
macro uncertainty. Nevertheless, we reiterate Buy on GCE as a top ASIC server PCB 
maker, with all-time focus on high-end AI server/networking and expanding margins.

Key Metrics      
* For full definitions of iQmethod SM measures, see page 7.

Company Sector 
Electronics

Company Description 
Gold Circuit (GCE) is a PCB company in Taiwan with high 
exposure in server (74% of 2025 sales) and networking 
(15%). Key end-clients are global CSPs and US top 
networking/telecom names. Besides its headquarters, the 
company has production sites in Taoyuan (Taiwan), Suzhou 
(China), Jiangsu (China), and Thailand, with ongoing capacity 
expansion. GCE was established in 1981 and listed on the 
Taiwan stock market in March 1998.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Gold Circuit, given (1) its high 
exposure and focus on better-margin server/networking 
business amid rising AI applications globally to keep high 
ASP/margin, (2) strong competitive advantages and high 
entry barrier as focusing on high-end products, (3) higher 
production flexibility after adding capacity in Thailand, (4) 
positive FCF/OCF since 2007/2019, respectively, with an 
improving balance sheet (from net debt to net cash) and 
expanding ROE.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
Gold Circuit (GOCCF) 
Our PO of NT$1,250 is based on 21x 2027E EPS. The 21x valuation multiple is around 
1.5SD above the average level since 2020 when better-margin server business started to 
account for 50%+ of total sales, leading to an improved 20%+ GM profile. We believe 
the P/E multiple target can be justified by (1) strong AI PCB market demand, (2) GCE's 
high AI server exposure, (3) solid sales mix from better-margin server/networking 
business, and (4) ongoing margin expansion to historical high level.

Upside risks: (1) stronger server/networking market demand to drive ASP/margins, (2) 
better utilization rate, (3) faster capacity expansion in Thailand, (4) lower raw 
material/labor/logistics costs, and (5) favorable FX movement.

Downside risks: (1) ASIC platform delay, (2) weaker market demand amid inflation/macro 
uncertainties, (3) softer AI server demand due to AI chip short supply, (4) GM pressure 
from lower utilization rate, higher raw material/labor/logistics costs, and (5) unfavorable 
FX movement.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line

We introduce 2027 forecast at 90.4K racks (+10% y/y) vs. our 2026 forecast
now at 82K racks: This year is a strong one for downstream rack assemblers, with
rack shipments up ~209% y/y, but we expect rack shipments to grow another ~10%
y/y to reach 90.4K in 2027. For 2027, our forecast implies Hon Hai will be a share
gainer with rack share up to 43% (from 37% in 2026), with Quanta staying
unchanged at 26% and Wistron's share up slightly to 24% (from 23% in 2026).

Quanta (2382.TW, OW): October revenue was NT$353.3bn (+9% m/m, +104% y/y).
We attribute the m/m growth mainly to higher GB200/300 rack shipments at ~1.8K
racks (+24% m/m), despite being offset by lower NB shipments at 2.7M units (-36%
m/m, -23% y/y). For 2026, we forecast Quanta to ship 20.9K racks, and our 2027
rack production forecast for Quanta is at 23.5K (+12% y/y).
Wistron (3231.TW, OW): October revenue was  NT$402.6B (+6% m/m, +108% y/y).
We attribute the m/m growth to: 1) higher GB200/300 compute tray shipments

(1.60K rack equivalents, by our estimates, up ~22% m/m), despite lower Wiwynn
revenue at ~NT$165.1bn (-4% m/m, +81% y/y) and lower NB shipments at 1.2mn units
(-20% m/m, -43% y/y), flat DT shipments at 500K (flat m/m, -44% y/y), and lower
monitor shipments at 850K (-11% m/m, -6% y/y).  For 2026, we forecast Wistron to
ship 18.7K racks, and our 2027 rack production forecast is at 22K (+18% y/y).
Hon Hai (2317.TW, OW): October revenue was NT$1,305.3bn (+13% m/m, +46% y/y),

with consumer segment revenue seeing the strongest m/m growth, followed by
computing products, and lastly, components and other products, while cloud and
networking products were down m/m due to a rise in consignment mix; however,
overall rack production volumes are up m/m. We model HH's October GB200/300
rack production at 2.9K (+7% m/m). For 2026, we forecast Foxconn to ship 30.3K
racks, and our rack production 2027 forecast at 38.8K (+28% y/y).

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We estimate GB200/300 rack output in October  at ~7.5K (+16% m/m).
Quanta shipped ~1.80K  GB200/GB300 racks.
Wistron shipped ~1.60K GB200/GB300 racks.
Hon Hai shipped ~2.90K GB200/300 racks.
We keep our 2027 rack production forecast unchanged at 90.4K racks (+11% y/y).

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
Base case scenario value, derived from a residual income valuation model. Our key assump-
tions include a cost of equity of 8.5%, a medium-term growth rate of 13%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected iPhone sell-through
n Faster progress in AI server business
n Faster EV business development progress
n Any new M&A activity that could improve sentiment

Risks to Downside
n Lower iPhone sell-through
n Smaller profit contribution from AI business
n Slower EV business development progress
n Geopolitical developments that could negatively affect foreign investment
Quanta Computer Inc. (2382.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.0% (beta of
1.2, equity premium of 6.0% and risk-free rate of 1.5%), an 8.5% medium-term growth rate, and
a 3% terminal growth rate.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected NB demand
n Stronger-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Stronger-than-expected server demand
n Faster-than-expected AI server penetration
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected NB demand
n Softer-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Weak margin performance owing to rising labor costs and sales shortfalls
n Fierce price competition in the mega data center segment
n Slower-than-expected AI server penetration

Wistron Corporation (3231.TW)
Base case, residual income valuation. Key assumptions: 8.7% cost of equity, 7.0% medium-
term growth rate and 3% terminal growth rate.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected divestiture of consumer electronics business
n Stronger-than-expected NB demand

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 1

More details on SBC — SBC's 9M26 sales declined by a single digit y/y with stable 
GPM and improving opex. Pre-tax profits rose 40% y/y in 9M26. Yet the higher 
effective tax rate dragged profitability, leading to a  net loss of US$25mn in 9M26. 
Mgmt expects SBC to launch more new products in 2027E which should further 
drive the top-line and earnings recovery.  
Implications — We expect a negative share price reaction to the results print given 
the weaker-than-expected core biz and the SBC loss. Mgmt expects FY26 sales to 
fall 14-15% y/y with 4Q OEM sales seasonally softer than 3Q. New models are due 
from Jan-27 and mgmt expects low-teens growth y/y for FY27E. We will look for 
more detail on the drivers of the expected sales recovery at its 3Q26 results briefing 
next Tuesday, Nov 17.

Merida (9914.TW)

3
Merida
Valuation
Our target price for Merida shares of NT$93 is based on a 2H26-1H27E P/E of 15.5x, a 30% discount to its historical average of
22x during pre-Covid years (2014-2019), in view of soft order momentum as a result of slow recovery in bike demand, a volatile
margin trend, and low visibility of SBC.
Risks
Downside risks that could mean the Merida shares trade below our target price include: 1) e-bike adoption slower than

expected; 2) weaker restocking demand; 3) lukewarm sell-through at SBC; and 4) a weaker macro.
Upside risks that could mean the Merida shares trade above our target price include: 1) e-bike adoption faster than expected;
2) stronger restocking demand; 3) stronger sell-through at SBC; and 4) a strong macro.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned

### Page 6

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 210: 261111_daiwa_merida

Source PDF: all report pdf/261111_daiwa_merida.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: Merida posted its 3Q26 results on 11 November after market hours. Merida’s 
gross margin came in at 15.2% (vs. 19.8% in 2Q26; 13.7% in 3Q25), lower than our 
estimate (16.2%) and the consensus (17.3%). Its 3Q26 EPS was TWD1.06, lower than our 
forecast (TWD1.68) and the consensus estimate (TWD1.65). Merida plans to hold an 
investor call on 17 November and we will provide an update then.

We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD96, based on a target PER of 17x 
applied on our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. For more information on this company, please 
refer to our latest flash note, 2Q26 review: mainly dragged by Specialized, on 14 August.

What’s the impact 
• 3Q26 results missed. Merida’s 3Q26 revenue of TWD6,098m (-12.1% QoQ, -15.6% 
YoY) missed our and the Bloomberg consensus estimates by 13.6% and 11.1%, 
respectively, which we attributed to the discount when destocking the older models. Its 
gross margin came in at 15.2% (vs. 19.8% in 2Q26; 13.7% in 3Q25), lower than our 
estimate (16.2%) and the consensus (17.3%), due to less OBM sales and the price 
discounting for further inventory digestion. Its operating profit of TWD384m (-49.8% 
QoQ, -9.7% YoY) also missed our and the consensus estimates by 35.2% and 37.3%, 
respectively. Its operating margin came in at 6.3% (vs. 11.0% in 2Q26; 5.9% in 3Q25), 
missing our estimate of 8.4% and the consensus of 8.9%. Considering the equity 
investment loss of TWD150m+, its net profit of TWD176m (-44.8% QoQ, -71.9% YoY) 
missed our and the consensus by 65.0% and 65.6%. Its 3Q26 EPS was TWD1.06, lower 
than our forecast (TWD1.68) and the consensus estimate (TWD1.65).

Merida: 3Q26 results vs. Daiwa and the Bloomberg estimates

Gross profit                                930               1,143  -18.7%        1,186  -21.6%

Source: Company, Bloomberg, Daiwa forecasts

• 4Q26 outlook. We forecast its 4Q26 revenue to decline QoQ as the company will 
continue to manage its existing models and expects new models in 2027 to bring 
stronger demand.

What we recommend 
We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD96, based on a target PER of 17x 
applied on our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. The stock is currently trading at PERs of 
13.3x/10.7x, based on our 2026/27E EPS, vs. its past-5-year one-year-forward PER trading 
range of 7-33x. Key downside risks: slower-than-expected e-bike penetration in the EU and 
the US, worse-than-expected demand in an inflationary environment and inventory digestion.

## Document 215: 261111_ml_kinik

Source PDF: all report pdf/261111_ml_kinik.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Kinik

Strong advanced nodes and packaging 
expansion support decent earnings

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 1,050 TWD | Price: 737.00 TWD

3Q26 margin beat on favorable product mix  
Kinik’s 3Q26 sales grew 8% QoQ, mainly driven by solid diamond disk demand from N2 
and advanced packaging capacity ramp and increasing shipments of high-end 
test/reclaim wafer, more than offsetting weakness at Thailand ABU subsidiary. 3Q26 
GMs improved by 220bps QoQ to 42.2% on improving product mix across ABU and DBU 
segments, taking 3Q26 EPS to NT$3.91(vs. BofAe/Street NT$4.09/NT$4.08).  
4Q26 sales grow on continued advanced nodes expansion 
The company guided 4Q26 sales to grow slightly QoQ (BofAe + 2% QoQ), implying full-
year revenue growth of +25% YoY, backed by strength across diamond disk, test wafer, 
and reclaim wafer amid ongoing advanced nodes capacity build-out. Strong contribution 
from high-margin DBU segment, coupled with a favorable mix shift in ABU driven by 
ABF diamond wheels, should lift 4Q26 GMs further to 43.5%, in our view.  
Solid DBU demand and SBU ramp underpin '27-28  
On accelerated 3nm, 2nm and A16 capacity ramp at full utilization, alongside benign 
mature node supply/demand landscape and steady engagement with Intel on 18A/14A, 
we forecast Kinik’s diamond disk shipment to +48% CAGR over '26-28. Meanwhile, 
Kinik’s SBU sales could deliver +22% CAGR over '26-28 as 10% price hike on selected 
products take effect from early 2027 and carrier wafer demand from Micron HBM grow 
more meaningfully from 2H27. Beyond its existing business, grinding wheels for 
advanced packaging are currently under customer qualification, and would provide 
upside potential with TAM much larger than diamond disk.  
Diverse drivers and margin expansion warrant re-rating 
We keep our 2026/27/28 EPS largely unchanged at NT$14.98/26.23/36.84. Reiterate our 
Buy rating and PO of NT$1,050, still based on 32x 2H27-1H28E P/E (vs. its historical 
range 9-42x), supported by robust sales/earnings CAGR of +32%/+57% over '26-28.

Company Sector 
Semiconductor Capital Equipment

Company Description 
Kinik was established in 1953 as a conventional grinding 
wheels supplier. Since 1990-2000s, the company has 
transformed itself into a semiconductor consumables 
supplier including CMP pad conditioners (diamond disks) 
and reclaim/test wafers.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Kinik. We acknowledge its 
technology for CMP diamond disk is solid to support its 
gain in TSMC advanced nodes and Intel along with growing 
reclaimed and testing wafer demand. We expect to see 
margins expansion from mix upgrade in coming years.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Kinik (KIKCF) 
Our Price Objective of NT$1,050 is based on 32x 2H27-1H28E P/E, which is at upper 
half of the company's historical trading range of 9-42x. In our view, the valuation 
multiple is underpinned by 
1) around +57% earnings CAGR in 2026-28E supported by +32% sales CAGR growth and 
GMs/OpMs expansion, 2) ROE expansion to around 30% in 2025-28E, 4) net cash of 
around NT$30/share, and 5) free cash flow generation of around NT$50/share per 
annum across 2025-27E.

Downside risks to our estimates and expectations are 
1) weaker market demand, especially in the cloud AI investment moderation, leading to 
lower utilization across its major foundry and memory customers and undermining the 
company's revenue growth and operating leverage 
2) slower manufacturing technology migration by its foundry and memory customers, 
dragging the product upgrade cycle for Kinik and potentially spurring more competition 
from its peers. 
3) failure to execute or delay of technology roadmap, potentially leading to an order shift 
to its competitors.

SK 하이닉스 (000660/KS) 
여전히 강력하다 
3Q26 영업이익 83 조원으로 예상 상회 
SK하이닉스의 3Q26 영업이익은 83조원 (+38% QoQ)으로 예상을 상회했다. 메모
리 공급 부족 심화, HBM4 반영 시작에 따른 메모리 가격 상승이 환율 하락을 상쇄했
기 때문이다. 출하 DRAM +10%, NAND -1%, 가격 DRAM +28%, NAND 
+24%, 영업이익률 DRAM 82%, NAND 72%, HBM 판가는 HBM4 출하가 시작되
며 37% 수준 상승한 것으로 추정된다. 업황은 여전히 예상을 상회하고 있다.

4Q26 분기 영업이익 100 조원 시대 진입 전망 
4Q26 영업이익은 105조원 (+27% QoQ)으로 분기 100조원 시대에 진입할 것으로 
전망한다. 메모리 가격 강세가 지속되는 가운데, 1c 출하 가속화, M15X 램프업 본격화 
등으로 출하량 상승이 기존 예상을 상회할 것이다. 또한 HBM4의 Cross-over 시작 
및 수율 안정화로 HBM 가격, 수익성 제고 동반이 지속될 것이다. 출하는 DRAM 
+24%, NAND +4%, 가격은 DRAM +8%, NAND +7%를 예상한다.

2027 년 영업이익 488 조원 (+70% YoY)으로 상향조정 
SK하이닉스의 2027년 영업이익을 488조원 (+70% YoY)으로 12% 상향 조정한
다. 원/달러 환율 전망치를 기존 1,340원에서 1,315원으로 하향 조정했음에도, 공급 
부족 심화 속 2027년향 HBM 가격 인상 및 예상을 상회하는 수율, 1Q27 진행될 모바
일 DRAM 가격 인상 등을 반영했기 때문이다. LTA 가격 역시 개별 약정된 범위 내 
시장 상황을 반영할 수 있는 구조라는 점을 감안하면, 지속 상승할 것이라 전망한다.

높은 주주환원 의지 확인, 높아진 실적 가시성, 지나친 저평가 
투자의견 매수, 목표주가 4,000,000원을 유지한다. SK하이닉스는 LTA에 대해 기
존 5년에서 추가 연장에 대한 논의가 진행 중이며, 2029년까지 HBM, DRAM은 사실
상 완판, HBM 수요는 공급 가능 물량의 2 배를 상회, 2030년까지 메모리 수요 우위 
국면 전망을 밝혔다. AI 인프라 투자의 강력한 명분과 메모리 수요의 구조성, 잠재적으
로 매우 높은 LTA 위반 비용을 감안하면 실적 가시성과 안정성 제고가 동반되는 국면
이다. 100조원 수준의 주주환원은 2026년 예상 FCF의 66% 수준, 분기별 자사주 
매입, 소각 지속 계획을 통해 높은 주주환원 의지를 보여주고 있다. 실적 Duration 장
기화, 주주환원을 감안하면 12MF P/E 3.4X 수준의 현 주가는 현저한 저평가 국면이
다. 과도한 우려의 되돌림과 메모리 밸류에 대한 재논의 국면 진입을 전망한다.

주가 추이

3Q26 매 출액 과 영 업 이익 은 비현 금 성 비용 항목 개 선 으로 시장 기대치
를 상 회 했 으나, 차량 판매 감소와 인 플 레 이션 등으로 연간 가이 던 스 는 
하 향 되 었 습니 다. 올해 생산 차질 에 따라 이 연된 신차 판 매를 ’27년에 
확대하며 매출/이익 회복을 가시 화 하 는 부분 에 주목 필 요 합니다. 
매출 성장은 금융부문이, 영업이익은 자동차부문이 기여 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 45.1조원(-3.5% 이하 Y oY), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%)을 기록하며 시장 기대치를 상회했다. 파업으로 인한 생산 차질
로 3Q 판매량 92.1만대(-11.3%)에 그치며 자동차부문 매출은 큰 폭으
로 감소(33조원, -10.0%)했으나, 금융부문 매출이 취급 자산 증가로 큰 
폭으로 성장(+27.8%)했다. 반면, 영업이익은 금리 상승에 따른 금융부문 
영업비용 확대 영향과 기타부문 실적 부진 발생과 달리 자동차부문은 전
년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소와 판매보증 충당부채 전입
액 감소 등 우호적 환율효과로 큰 폭의 증가세(+24.8%) 기록했다. 
’26년 가이던스 하향, 구조적 반등 요인은 아직 
3Q 실적 발표에서 주목할 부분은 가이던스 하향과 현금흐름이다. 연간 
판매목표를 연초 415.8만대에서 400만대로 하향했고 영업이익률 목표
는 6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 0.8%p 하향했으며, ’26년 투자계획은 
17.8조원에서 16조원으로 낮췄다. 특히, 투자계획 하향은 차량 판매 매
출 감소 대비 인센티브, 원자재 가격 상승 등으로 현금흐름이 악화된 
부분에 기인한 것으로 판단된다. 실적발표에서 전사적 원가 최적화 활
동 추진을 강조했으나, 이는 좀 더 장기적 측면에서 효과가 가시화될 
것으로 판단되며, 인플레이션 및 원화절상 상황 고려 시 구조적 반등은 
단기적으로 쉽지 않을 수 있다. 
절치부심의 2027년, 목표주가 600.000원 유지 
그러나 매크로 상황을 제 외 하 면  ’26년 하반기 생산 차질에 따른 신차 
판매효과가 ’27년 으 로 이연된 것 이 라 는 점에 주목 필요하다.   ‘북미 HEV, 유럽 
BEV’ 전략 하에서 엘란트라, 투싼 풀세인지, GV80, G80 등 고수익 HEV 차종 
중심 미국 수요 대응을 통해 Mix 개선하는 모습이 확인되고, 생산이 본 격 화 된 
아이오닉3 유럽 판매 확대 시 중국 로 컬 업 체 들 의 공세를 방 어 하 는 모 습 을 
확인하는 것이 가 능 할 것으로 판 단 한 다. 노 후 차 종 중심 운 영 에 따른 판매 둔화 
이외에 아직 근 원 적 판매 경쟁력 저하로는 판단하지 않는다.

이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

## Document 323: Anthropic IPO路演之际，OpenAI也披露财务数据：Q3收入不及，亏损远超

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic IPO路演之际，OpenAI也披露财务数据：Q3收入不及，亏损远超.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

OpenAI 三季度营收达 100 亿美元，季环⽐增⻓近 50% ，年化运⾏速率约 500 亿美元，但运
营亏损仍从 101 亿扩⼤⾄ 109 亿美元。竞争对⼿ Anthropic 启动 IPO 路演，三季度营收 170 亿
美元，亏损仅 5.67 亿，在体量与盈利能⼒上均领先。
摘要：
OpenAI 最新财务数据显⽰，其营收增⻓势头强劲，但盈利⽬标依然遥远。
11 ⽉ 10 ⽇，据彭博社援引知情⼈⼠透露，OpenAI 三季度（截⾄ 9 ⽉末）营收达 100 亿美元，季
环⽐增⻓近 50% ，同⽐增幅接近三倍。
调整后运营利润率从 -150% 则从改善⾄ -109% 。尽管利润率有所提升，但绝对亏损⾦额仍⼩幅扩
⼤，从 101 亿美元升⾄ 109 亿美元。
上述财务数据于周⼆在⼀场投资者电话会议中披露。CFO Sarah Friar 将这⼀季度定性为 " 爆炸
性季度 " ，并透露公司年化营收运⾏速率已达约 500 亿美元。
竞争对⼿ Anthropic 正于同⽇启动 IPO 路演。据 Anthropic 此前披露，其第三季度营收已达 170 亿

美元，同期运营亏损仅 5.67 亿美元，⽆论在体量还是盈利能⼒上均⼤幅领先 OpenAI 。
OpenAI 营收⾼增⻓难掩亏损扩⼤，继续加码企业市场
据 Sarah Friar 介绍，三季度营收增⻓的核⼼驱动⼒来⾃前沿研究和企业业务。
她表⽰， OpenAI 企业业务单季度实现翻倍增⻓，推动企业端与消费者端收⼊⽐例趋近于五五
开。在具体产品层⾯， Friar 点名了编程⼯具 Coding 、⽹络安全产品 Cyber 以及企业 AI 智能体平台
Dots 。她说：
中⼩企业业务同样录得加速，三季度增速超过此前六个季度的总和。然⽽，运营亏损从 101 亿美
元⼩幅上升⾄ 109 亿美元，表明公司在控制成本⽅⾯仍⾯临较⼤压⼒。
OpenAI 正将更多资源押注于利润率更⾼的企业市场，以缩⼩与 Anthropic 之间的差距。Friar 坦
⾔， Anthropic 在企业端的成功为 OpenAI 提供了重要的战略参照。
她表⽰，此前公司在微软 Azure 合同框架内受到约束，限制了与第三⽅云服务商开展分发合作的

空间。 Friar 说：
为强化这⼀战略，公司近期任命 Dali Rajic 出任⾸席营收官，专职负责企业业务板块的拓展。企
业市场在 OpenAI 整体营收中的占⽐正持续提升。
正是这些产品在企业端加速落地，推动了业绩增⻓。
我认为将这⼀限制解开之后， Anthropic 向我展⽰了这种模式的强⼤⼒量，对我们⽽⾔，这在
2027 年可以成为⾮常重要的增⻓顺⻛。

写评论
消费者端⽅⾯，Friar 披露消费者业务季环⽐销售额提升 20% ，付费订阅⽤⼾数突破 1 亿。公司整
体每周活跃⽤⼾达 12 亿。值得注意的是，与 OpenAI 不同， Anthropic 不设消费端业务，增⻓完全
依赖企业市场。
推出商业主⻚功能，剑指⾕歌本地搜索
在产品层⾯，华尔街⻅闻提及，周⼆公司宣布推出 "Businesses in ChatGPT" 测试版，允许企业
认领并管理其在 ChatGPT 上的商业主⻚，追踪⽤⼾互动数据，并通过专属 AI 代理与潜在客⼾直
接对话。
符合条件的企业还可创建专属的业务代理（ Business Agent ），使 ChatGPT ⽤⼾能够直接向其提
问，获取关于该企业及其产品的信息。
此外，商业中⼼还将向企业提供有限度的数据洞察，涵盖个⼈主⻚浏览量、品牌提及次数、⽹

站点击量等指标，这⼀设计令⼈联想到⾕歌分析（ Google Analytics ）的功能定位。
在验证机制⽅⾯，企业须经过正式流程完成⾝份核验。 OpenAI 货币化副总裁 Benji Shomair 表
⽰，公司针对不同市场、不同类型的企业采⽤多种验证⽅式，但未披露具体细节。
双雄竞速上市，估值⻆⼒进⼊关键窗⼝
融资与上市进程为两家公司的竞争增添了新的维度。
华尔街⻅闻提及， Anthropic 已于周⼆启动 IPO 路演，计划上市时间为 11 ⽉ 19 ⽇或 20 ⽇，⽬标估
值约为 1.4 万亿美元。
OpenAI 则选择将 IPO 计划推迟⾄明年，⽬前正以 1.25 万亿美元估值推进新⼀轮融资，已完成⾸
批 110 亿美元的股权募集。公司表⽰，所筹资⾦将⽤于⽀撑持续⾼强度的资本开⽀需求。
尽管两家公司在营收确认⽅式上存在差异，但三季度的业绩对⽐显⽰出明显的收敛态势 ——
Anthropic 以约 170 亿美元的绝对营收保持领先， OpenAI 则以更快的加速度弥合差距。
在各⾃ IPO 叙事成型的关键节点，这场营收竞赛的⾛势将直接影响投资者对两家公司⻓期商业潜

⼒的定价判断。

## Document 352: Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

Central banks: a pause while waiting for more information

As expected, the Fed, the ECB, and the BoJ left their rates unchanged this week. This pause , however, does not mark 
the end of the cycle. The ECB’s tone is becoming more balanced, weighing inflationary risks against tightening financial 
conditions, but one final rate hike in December remains our base case. Across the Atlantic, Kevin Warsh describes an 
environment conducive to further tightening, while remaining cautious about the risk of a credit shock. In Japan, the 
lasting exit from the deflationary cycle is taking shape, and monetary policy adjustment is expected to continue 
gradually. We anticipate two rate hikes by the Fed by January and four by the BoJ by the second half of 2028.

Eurozone: A more balanced tone, but the course remains unchanged. The ECB’s statement marks a shift from September. 
It now explicitly acknowledges both upside risks to inflation (the magnitude and persistence of the energy shock) and downsid e 
pressures (tightening financial conditions). The statement that inflation would  remain “well above target for an extended period” 
no longer appears. In essence, however, there is little change. A rate hike in December remains our base case scenario, widel y 
anticipated and implicitly signaled by Christine Lagarde during the Q&A (“If the current situation is not called into question by the 
next set of economic data, the path is clear”). Lagarde presented the rise in sovereign yields as a global phenomenon that is  
tightening financial conditions; regarding the TPI, she stuck to the usual stance: the ECB will intervene if monetary policy 
transmission were to be hampered “by disorderly movements not justified by fundamentals” . By December, the indirect and 
second-round effects of the energy shock will be closely scrutini sed. From our perspective, September’s producer prices offer 
clearer signals, but the increase remains limited to a few sectors: energy, electricity-intensive industries and electrical equipment, 
driven by IA 1. For now, companies in the Eurozone, particularly in the services sector, appear to be absorbing the shock. The 
European Commission’s survey released this Thursday indicates a rise in the sales price index in October, which remains in line 
with recent months.

United States: The Fed navigates between risks. The FOMC pauses after September’s rate hike and maintains the federal 
funds target at 3.75%-4.0%. However, Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh outlined a landscape conducive to further tightening: stronger 
growth, “rather positive” labor market conditions, and, above all, inflation that is “too high.” As a result, two dissenting votes (out 
of 12) were cast in favor of a 25bps hike.  But the committee also appears to fear the adverse consequences of tightening too 
quickly, which is why it is waiting to “accumulate more information.” AI is increasingly at the heart of monetary policymaking. In 
particular, K. Warsh warned against the risks of an abrupt tightening of interest rate conditions, including for the AI investment 
cycle - which triggered a steepening of the yield curve. He also announced that he would soon (on November 16) attend a hearing 
of the “Productivity and Employment” task force to “refine his views” on the implications of AI for the dual mandate. Governor Lisa 
Cook, who voted for a 25bps hike, had noted ahead of the committee meeting “ongoing inflationary pressures” and a risk that they 
might not “subside quickly”. In addition, K. Warsh announced that there would be fewer meetings resulting in rate decisions: 7 in 
2027 and 6 in 2028, compared with 8 today. The change is expected to take effect starting in the fall of 2027. The meetings freed 
up by this change would allow the committee to focus on “strategic deliberations” and long-term issues.

Japan: A status quo, but a tightening cycle set to continue as the economy emerges from deflation. The Bank of Japan 
decided, in line with our forecasts and the consensus, to keep its policy rate target steady at 1.25% at its October 29-30 meeting. 
One dissenting vote called for a rate hike (25bps), contrasting with the two dovish dissenting votes (opposed to a rate hike) at the 
previous meeting. As in the US, t he decision is justified by the need to further analy se risks to economic activity, prices, and 
financial conditions. Above all, the central bank’s outlook for economic activity and prices marks a turning point: it explicitly states 
that “core inflation has reached a level generally in line with price stability” and that it is “expected to remain there.” Governor Ueda 
refined this by noting that it is now necessary to “anchor” inflation at this level before declaring the target achieved. Furthermore, 
the emphasis on “upside risks to prices” and the fact that an y rise in core inflation is now deemed “undesirable” indicate that 
monetary tightening will continue.

The monetary tightening cycle continues. The next steps are likely to be more significant in the United States, where inflation 
has exceeded the target for nearly six years and coexists with a favorable outlook for growth and employment. In the Eurozone, 
the rise in inflation is primarily due to a supply shock in energy costs (though it is also fueled by demand in the United St ates). It 
continues to have little impact on measures of core inflation, including those tracked by the ECB, such as the H ICP; and 
households’ long-term inflation expectations remain anchored. The degree of monetary tightening also differs. In real terms, the 
ECB’s current rates (the deposit facility rate deflated by the core CPI) hover around 0%, a level that more or less corresponds to 
estimates of the natural rate. The Fed, meanwhile, remains in accommodative territory: the real rate (Fed Funds deflated by Core

1 This is a point underscored by the first PPI releases for September (Spain, Italy).

### Page 2

PCE) is at around 0.8%, 40bps below the committee’s implied long-term level (1.2%). Japan remains an outlier: the rise in rates 
preceded the inflationary shock and, until now, has been more a matter of normalization. It is therefore more extensive and spread 
out over time. The central bank is adjusting to the new inflation regime and remains cautious in its exit from accommodative 
policies. The starting point is significantly lower, with real rates still negative, but the adjustment cycle should ultimately bring it to 
the upper end of the neutral range estimated by the BoJ (1.1% -2.5%). We therefore anticipate one final rate hike by the ECB in 
December, two more hikes by the Fed, first in December and then in January, and four spaced-out hikes by the BoJ through the 
second half of 2028.

Anis Bensaidani   
Economist, United States, Japan 
anis.bensaidani@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

## Document 353: Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

By Advanced Economies Team
Advanced Economies:
Resilience to shock after shock
October 2026

### Page 2

• The global economy is in the midst of a growth cycle. As a result, GDP growth is more resilient to shocks than initially
anticipated.
• The figures for the second quarter bear this out, despite the sharp rise in oil prices, whose negative impact on growth is offset
by a series of reflationary forces.
• The driving force behind the current growth cycle is clear: artificial intelligence (AI).
• The world’s major economies are currently engaged in a broader push to expand their capital goods investment.

• While European and US households are experiencing more or less the same energy price shock, inflation and household
consumption remain higher in the US.
• Higher and above-target inflation calls for monetary tightening.
• Inflation is exceeding the target set by three of the four central banks examined in this publication (the US, the UK, the
Eurozone). Inflation is currently expected to peak in Q4 2026 or Q1 2027, but there is a significant risk that energy inflation will

spread further to other components of inflation in the future.
• Inflation can also be a sign of (too much) economic strength (in the US).
• Monetary policies also have to offset the inflationary effects of fiscal policy (particularly in Japan).
• The outbreak of the conflict in Iran acted as a catalyst: the ensuing rise in energy prices contributes to the recent increase in
long-term rates (which had been trending downwards at the start of the year) to levels close to those of 2002.

• The other issue is the high level of fiscal deficits (too high for a period of growth), reflecting different fiscal trajectories. In the
US, the UK, France and Germany the public debt-to-GDP ratio is increasing, while it has stabilized in Italy and decreased in
Spain and Japan.
• The rise in long-term interest rates will weigh on public finances. The fiscal arithmetic is simpler today than it will be in 2030.
By 2030, the effective rate will have risen and caught up with GDP growth.

For more information, please refer to our publication:
EcoPerspectives – Advanced Economies | 3rd quarter of 2026, as of September 24, 2026– Economic Research– BNP Paribas
Advanced economies: Resilience to shock after shock

## Document 446: Sea三季度净利增长34%：电商增强，金融临考

Source PDF: all report pdf/Sea三季度净利增长34%：电商增强，金融临考.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

11 ⽉ 10 ⽇， Sea (SE.US) 公布截⾄ 2026 年 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇的第三季度未经审计财务业绩。 GAAP 收⼊

## Document 471: Tracking Europe’s AI transition_ A new STOXX 600 earnings-call framework (2026-11-11)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Tracking Europe’s AI transition_ A new STOXX 600 earnings-call framework (2026-11-11).pdf
Report source: DB Research
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

Tracking Europe’s AI transition:  
A new STOXX 600 earnings-call 
framework

1 
Authors 
Michael Kirker 
Economist

November 11, 2026 
How quickly is AI moving from experimentation into everyday business, and 
where are companies reporting tangible gains? The answers matter for 
institutional investors and policymakers assessing productivity, operating 
efficiency and labour demand. Yet comparable evidence across Europe remains 
limited, making it difficult to track the pace and scale of the transition. 1 
We address this gap with a proprietary framework that turns what is said on 
STOXX 600 earnings call into three structured indicators: AI adoption 
intensity, reported productivity benefits, and the impact on workforces. 
Large language models analyse what management teams say about AI’s 
operational role, applying a common scoring framework across companies, 
sectors and countries. Our new approach provides a timely pan-European 
comparable tracker that captures not just whether AI is being mentioned, but its 
real-world impact as the adoption of the new technology unfolds. 
Key Takeaways: 
— Adoption is broadening, with signs of faster progress. AI-use scores rose 
gradually from a low base before strengthening over the past 12 months. The 
increase extends across sectors, although the pace and level of adoption 
remain uneven. 
— Workforce impacts remain limited in corporate disclosures. The 
framework does not show a broad negative workforce signal attributable to 
AI. The aggregate score remains close to the level of no impact on the labour 
force across all of our sample. However, this does not rule out AI displacing 
workers at a future date. 
— Geographic differences persist within the listed-company sample. Since 
2022, German companies in the STOXX 600 have generally recorded above-
average AI-use and reported productivity scores. Dutch and Spanish 
companies in the STOXX 600 have tended to score lower on AI use, although 
Spain has recently gone through a rapid catch up. 
— The technology J-Curve: The findings are consistent with an early stage of 
the AI transition. Companies report increasing adoption of the new 
technology, but transformational productivity gains and extensive workforce 
restructuring remain uncommon.

1 1 Our US team regularly publish on data from the USCB showing the impact of 
AI on businesses, and our German team have recently begun their own 
chartbook. In the UK, the Office for National Statistics publishes on the topic too.

### Page 2

Deutsche Bank AG Page 2

Methodology 
Sample period: Our sample begins in January 2022, providing a baseline before 
ChatGPT’s launch on 30 November 2022. AI in this framework covers both 
established and emerging technologies, so the launch of ChatGPT is a reference 
point rather than the start of all corporate AI adoption. 
Timing of observations: STOXX 600 companies report on different financial 
calendars and at quarterly or semi -annual intervals. Their earnings calls also mix 
discussion of completed activity with forward-looking guidance. 
We assign each transcript to the calendar quarter in which the earnings call takes 
place, regardless of the financial reporting period discussed. The resulting 
indicators therefore track when information about AI use and its operational 
impacts becomes public rather than when implementation or the reported effect 
occurred. This provides a common dating convention across the sample while 
retaining differences in reporting frequency and disclosure timing.

Figure 1: Coverage of the STOXX 600 earnings calls

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: Share of the STOXX 600 is based on the current company weight in the index. Data collection for the current quarter (Q4 2026) is incomplete 
as some companies are still to report

Constructing the STOXX 600 AI corpus : We use a specialised AI transcription 
engine to process earnings calls from STOXX 600 companies, restricting the 
analysis to calls for which we obtain complete transcripts of sufficient accuracy. 
In Q3 2026, the latest full quarter, the dataset covered more than 500 companies, 
representing approximately 90% of the index’s current market capitalisation 
weights (Figure 1). In the current quarter (Q4) earnings calls are still taking place. 
Nearly 250 companies with an equivalent weight to around c.60% of the STOXX 
index having already reported. 
Historical coverage typically ranges from 300 to 500 companies and represents 
more than 80% of the index’s current market -capitalisation weights. Coverage 
peaks twice a year because some European companies report semi-annually.  
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 3

Deutsche Bank AG Page 3

### Page 4

Deutsche Bank AG Page 4

For examples of the AI’s scoring and justifications, see the tables presented in the 
Annex at the end of this report. 
Summary statistics 
Figure 2 shows the distribution of scores across all earnings-call transcripts in the 
historical sample, without weighting for company size or reporting frequency. 
These are transcript-level distributions rather than shares of companies, and they 
combine early and recent observations. They provide context for the quarterly 
indicators: 
• AI use score : Approximately 60% of earnings call transcripts do not 
discuss AI use. Among the minority that do discuss it, most fall into the 
“Active implementation” range (a score of 4-6 out of 10). 
• AI’s productivity impact: Nearly 70% of earnings calls do not mention a 
tangible AI-related productivity benefit. Among calls that do, scores are 
generally in the medium -impact band, indicating notable operational 
improvements. Transformational gains, scored at 8 or above out of 10 , 
are rarely reported. 
• AI’s labour impact: Only around 10% of earnings calls discuss AI -related 
workforce adjustments. Reported changes typically receive scores of 1 –
2 in magnitude on the −5 to +5 scale, with a slight tilt towards hiring and 
upskilling rather than redundancies.

Figure 2: Raw distribution of scores across the entire sample

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Note: Results are based on the raw number of transcripts, and are independent of firm size, and biased towards firms reporting more often.

Taken together, the distributions are consistent with an early adoption phase: 
companies discuss AI implementation more often than transformational 
productivity gains or extensive workforce restructuring. A technology J -curve 
offers one interpretation of this pattern,  with investment and organisational 
adaptation preceding broader benefits.  
This J-curve pattern is strongly corroborated by external micro-data, such as the 
Banco de España’s business activity survey  (EBAE) of Spanish firms, which 
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 5

Deutsche Bank AG Page 5

similarly indicates that AI has a more immediate impact on upfront investment 
and productivity than on headcount. 
AI use: A broad-based rise, with signs of faster recent adoption 
The new AI -use indicator points to a broadening transition across STOXX 600 
companies, with early signs that adoption has accelerated recently. Scores rose 
only gradually from 2022  and 2024 , but the latest observations suggest a 
stronger pace across industries and country groups.  
The rise is broad -based rather than confined to one sector. Technology has led 
much of the sample, while financials show tentative recent outperformance and 
utilities have caught up from a low starting point. Among the large country groups, 
German companies have persistently recorded above-average scores.  
AI use across European industries 
To trace sectoral dispersion,  we group companies by STOXX industry 
classification and calculate a weighted average of their scores for each calendar 
quarter, using their latest STOXX 600 index weights. 2  
We focus on four sectors spanning different business models: financials, the 
largest services sector; industrials, the largest physical-assets sector; technology, 
a likely early adopter; and utilities, an infrastructure-heavy sector where adoption 
may proceed more slowly.  
Figure 3 presents the AI use score for these four key industries alongside the 
range for all industry scores (represented by the shaded area). 
• The AI use score is general ly low over time but is gradually rising from 
around 1 point in 2022 to around 3 in 2026. 
• AI-use scores are rising across industries, but differences between 
sectors are widening. The common direction therefore points to 
broadening adoption rather than uniform progress: sectors can 
participate in the transition while remaining at different levels of use. 
In terms of the industries of interest: 
• Technology’s early lead has narrowed.  The sector’s AI -use score was 
near the top of the industry range through to 2024  but has recently 
plateaued as the broader STOXX 600 average caught up during 2025 –
26.3 
• Financials and industrials broadly track the index average. Both sectors 
have remained close to the aggregate AI -use score  over history, with 
financials generally a little below average, and industrials a little above. 
But financials show tentative signs of moving above average in recent 
quarters.

2 The industries are: (1) Basic Materials , (2), Consumer Discretionary , (3), Consumer Staples , (4) Energy, (5) 
Financials, (6) Health Care, (7) Industrials, (9) Real Estate, (10) Technology, (11) Telecommunications, (12) 
Utilities. 
3 Q4 2025 saw a large increase in the AI use score for the “Technology” industry, jumping up to around 6. This 
one-off spike was driven by ASML’s announcement of a significant strategic partnership with and investment 
in Mistral AI ” to 'embed AI across our entire holistic portfolio in order to increase the performance and 
productivity of our systems and the yield of our customers' processes' . Because of ASML’s large market cap 
(around 49% of our Technology category), it distorts the weighted average significantly.

Focus Europe

### Page 6

Deutsche Bank AG Page 6

• Utilities have caught up from a low base.  After recording lower AI -use 
scores than other sectors for much of the sample, utilities converged 
towards the STOXX 600 average during 2026. The result shows that the 
rise in reported adoption extends beyond the sectors most closely 
associated with AI.

Figure 3: AI use score (0-10) by industry

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The value for each industry is computed as a weighted average of all firms within the industry where the weights are derived from the current 
STOXX 600 share of each company. The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

As a cross -check, our findings align with the European Commission’s October 
Eurobarometer Flash survey of MSEs and Mid -Caps and the Eurostat annual ICT 
surveys, which show low overall intensity but a similar expansion in service - and 
tech-related sectors.  
AI use across countries 
Companies in the STOXX 600 tend to be large multi -nationals. That makes it 
difficult to pin down where physically the AI transformation is taking place within 
their organisation. Instead, our country comparison is done using two 
classifications of a company’s country: 
• Country of domicile: AI is a transformation technology, and this type of 
change is likely to be driven in organisation from the top down. We 
therefore consider a company’s country as that in which it is domiciled 
(which is likely to be where the headquarters is also). 
• Country of primary listing:  While companies can generally list in other 
countries, there tends to be a home bias in their choice of exchange.  
Figure 4 presents the AI use intensity metric by both of these measures of country 
(the company’s domicile and primary exchange listing ). Again, the shaded area 
shows the range across all classifications, with the largest countries/exchanges 
singled out. 
German-domiciled companies and the Xetra -listed (Frankfurt stock exchange) 
group record above-average AI-use scores in most quarters since 2022.  
Spain’s AI-use score has risen noticeably over recent quarters and now exceeds 
the aggregate average, reversing its earlier below-average position.  
11 November 2026
Focus Europe

### Page 7

Deutsche Bank AG Page 7

AI-use scores rise across country groups, with indications of faster growth from 
2025. The shared direction suggests a broad European listed -company trend, 
even though differences in score levels and timing persist between countries.

Figure 4: AI use intensity (0-10) by country

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

As a geographical cross -check, our index matches the broad trends of the 
Eurostat ICT annual survey, which places Germany as a clear frontrunner among 
its large peers. But some differences do exist. For instance, Eurostat shows the 
Netherlands with a very high AI adoption.  As Figure 5 demonstrates, there is a 
positive correlation between the AI use score metric derived here, and that from 
Eurostat, even though the Eurostat data measures if firms are adoptin g AI, and 
our new use measure also incorporates the concept of how intensively firms are 
using AI.

AI productivity impact: Reported benefits are rising, but 
transformational gains remain uncommon 
Higher intensity of use does not necessarily mean firms are benefiting more from 
AI. But our AI productivity impac t metric does show a similar pattern to our AI 
usage metric, although it tends to be slightly lower, indicating that companies 
may not yet be reaping the full rewards for their AI investment. 
Financials stand out more clearly on reported productivity benefits than on AI-use 
intensity, while German companies again record relatively strong scores , but only 
in more recent history.

Figure 6: AI's impact on productivity (0-10) by industry

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

AI productivity impact across European industries 
Reported AI -productivity benefits have become more prominent across 
industries. Figure 6 shows scores rising gradually during 2022 –25, followed by 
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 9

Deutsche Bank AG Page 9

indications of a faster increase from late 2025. The profile resembles the AI -use 
trend in Figure 4, suggesting that the stronger adoption narrative is accompanied 
by more discussion of operational gains. 
The aggregate trajectories of the AI use and AI productivity impact scores are 
similar, but sector rankings differ. Technology’s early relative strength in AI use is 
not matched by its productivity score, while financials report above-average 
productivity benefits despite more modest use scores. 
AI productivity impact across countries 
Figure 7 plots the self -report impact of AI on company productivity by country. 
Between 2022 -23, German companies record ed average productivity impact, 
even though their AI use score was relatively high. However more recently, their 
AI impact score has persistently been above average.  The UK grouping shows 
persistent early outperformance in AI impact during 2022–23, followed by 
convergence towards the aggregate average.

Figure 7: AI's impact on productivity (0-10) by country

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

Figure 8 shows that there is a strong positive correlation between the AI use score 
and the AI productivity score across the STOXX 600 ( r-squared = 0.8 3). The 
relationship between the scores is approximately linear, with a slope of around

0.66: a one-point higher use score is associated with roughly a 0. 66-point higher
